- **b-cache-optimizer**: Use when optimizing Anthropic prompt caching in MindBridge, debugging low cache hit rates, or structuring the system prompt and patient context blocks for maximum cache reuse. Covers the 5-minute TTL constraint, ephemeral cache_control, and how to measure cache savings on Message.cachedInputTokens. ([Dr-Snatch/mindbridge-hackathon](https://github.com/Dr-Snatch/mindbridge-hackathon/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/b-cache-optimizer.md))
- **b-coping-injector**: Use when building the coping plan retrieval and injection system. Covers matching a patient's current message to their therapist-written coping plans, the embedding-based retrieval pipeline, the MVP LIKE-match fallback, and how plans get woven into the conversation agent's context block without feeling clinical. ([Dr-Snatch/mindbridge-hackathon](https://github.com/Dr-Snatch/mindbridge-hackathon/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/b-coping-injector.md))
- **b-crisis-detector**: Use when building, testing, or updating the MindBridge crisis detection system. Covers the deterministic lexicon, regex patterns, the Haiku async backup classifier, canned response text, and flag emission. This is the highest-priority component in the entire system — it ships before anything else. ([Dr-Snatch/mindbridge-hackathon](https://github.com/Dr-Snatch/mindbridge-hackathon/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/b-crisis-detector.md))
- **b-pattern-detector**: Use when building or debugging the MindBridge cognitive pattern detection system. Covers detecting rumination, catastrophising, anxiety spirals, mind-reading, and withdrawal from patient messages. Implements the two-tier approach: deterministic keyword pass first, then Haiku judge for ambiguous cases. ([Dr-Snatch/mindbridge-hackathon](https://github.com/Dr-Snatch/mindbridge-hackathon/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/b-pattern-detector.md))
- **b-prompt-engineer**: Use when designing or refining the MindBridge conversation agent's system prompt. Covers the core persona (warm, validating, CBT-informed reframing), the patient context block structure, prompt caching strategy, disclaimer injection, and the prohibited outputs list. Also handles the CRISIS_MODE system prompt variant. ([Dr-Snatch/mindbridge-hackathon](https://github.com/Dr-Snatch/mindbridge-hackathon/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/b-prompt-engineer.md))
- **b-safety-auditor**: Use when reviewing AI outputs, prompts, or system design for mental health safety compliance. Checks for prohibited outputs (diagnoses, medication advice, discouraging professional help), validates the layered safety architecture is correctly implemented, and reviews lexicon changes. Should be run before any demo or deployment. ([Dr-Snatch/mindbridge-hackathon](https://github.com/Dr-Snatch/mindbridge-hackathon/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/b-safety-auditor.md))
